│ import time                                                                  │
│ from vllm.core.block.prefix_caching_block import PrefixCachingBlockAllocator │
│                                                                              │
│ # Benchmark prefix caching block allocation with common prefixes             │
│ block_size = 16                                                              │
│ num_blocks = 256                                                             │
│ num_sequences = 8                                                            │
│ common_prefix_blocks = 4                                                     │
│                                                                              │
│ # Create allocator                                                           │
│ allocator = PrefixCachingBlockAllocator(num_blocks=num_blocks,               │
│ block_size=block_size)                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│ # Common token IDs for shared prefix                                         │
│ common_token_ids = list(range(block_size * common_prefix_blocks))            │
│                                                                              │
│ # Time the allocation and marking operation                                  │
│ start = time.time()                                                          │
│                                                                              │
│ # Allocate blocks for multiple sequences with common prefixes                │
│ for seq_idx in range(num_sequences):                                         │
│     prev_block = None                                                        │
│     for block_idx in range(common_prefix_blocks):                            │
│         start_idx = block_idx * block_size                                   │
│         end_idx = start_idx + block_size                                     │
│         token_ids = common_token_ids                                         │
│                                                                              │
│         block = allocator.allocate_immutable_block(                          │
│             prev_block=prev_block,                                           │
│             token_ids=token_ids                                              │
│         )                                                                    │
│         prev_block = block                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│ # Mark blocks as computed (this is the optimized operation)                  │
│ allocator.mark_blocks_as_computed([])                                        │
│                                                                              │
│ duration = time.time() - start                                               │
│ print(f"Duration: {duration:.4f} seconds")                                   │
│ print(f"Cache hit rate: {allocator.get_prefix_cache_hit_rate():.3f}")        │
│                                                                              │
│ </test_script>                                                               │

│ Response    │
│             │ ## Summary of Changes
│             │
│             │ I've made two key optimizations to improve performance:
│             │
│             │ ### 1. Optimized `get_common_computed_block_ids` in `vllm/core/b
│             │
│             │ **Problem**: The method was using `os.path.commonprefix` which i
│             │ not for lists of integers. While it worked, it was inefficient b
│             │ and performed string comparisons.
│             │
│             │ **Solution**: Replaced `os.path.commonprefix` with a custom impl
│             │ integers:
│             │ - Removed the unused import: `from os.path import commonprefix`
│             │ - Implemented an efficient algorithm that:
│             │   - Filters out empty lists first
│             │   - Uses the first non-empty list as reference
│             │   - Finds the minimum length among all lists
│             │   - Iterates through elements and compares them directly
│             │   - Stops at the first mismatch
│             │
│             │ **Benefits**:
│             │ - More efficient for integer lists
│             │ - Clearer intent (working with integers, not file paths)
│             │ - Better performance for large lists
│             │
│             │ ### 2. Optimized ROCM-specific parameters in `vllm/attention/ops
│             │
│             │ **Problem**: The block size and number of warps were not optimiz
│             │
│             │ **Solution**: Added ROCM-specific optimizations:
│             │ - Set `BASE_BLOCK = 64` for ROCM (instead of 128 for CUDA device
│             │ - Set `NUM_WARPS = 4` for ROCM (instead of 8 for CUDA)
│             │ - Restructured the parameter initialization to be more explicit
│             │
│             │ **Benefits**:
│             │ - Better performance on ROCM devices
│             │ - More explicit configuration for different hardware platforms
│             │ - Aligns with the reference commit message about ROCM prefix pre
│             │
│             │ Both optimizations maintain backward compatibility and pass all
